# Agents · S · part 40

973 entries, spec-reviewer to spec_partner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews one repo's finished stages of a cross-repo spec against the spec and its handoff, read-only. Use when the user says "&lt;repo&gt; work is done, can you review?" before writing the next session's prompt. The prompt must name the repo, the spec, and the branch or PR. ([Luis-Palacios/members-management-workspace](https://github.com/Luis-Palacios/members-management-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only, separate-context spec reviewer. It audits an atomic spec + its acceptance contract for SHAPE and requirement quality against the industry-grounded template — EARS-phrased singular/measurable acceptance criteria, the Requirement (triggered) vs Invariant (always-true) split, prior-art grounding, scope/out-of-scope, and flagged uncertainty — and emits categorized findings (Block / Risk / Nit) for the operator. Use it when a spec has been drafted/refined and you want a fresh review pass the authoring context lacks. This is the SPEC SHAPE + requirement-quality lens — NOT the code lens (… ([lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry](https://github.com/lukasrepublic/agentic-foundry/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Prüft den Diff eines Branches in frischem Kontext gegen Story und Change-Ordner — jedes Akzeptanzkriterium umgesetzt und getestet, kein Scope Creep, Delta eingepflegt, Feature-Datei nachgeführt, nichts nur im Ticket spezifiziert. Einsetzen in /flow-implement Schritt 9 für jede Story und jeden Bugfix. Mitgeben — Issue-Nummer und Pfade, nicht die Begründungen des Implementierers. ([LukasW/PIckel](https://github.com/LukasW/PIckel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 定向只读核对实现是否满足用户要求和已批准契约。 ([lurenxing628/----](https://github.com/lurenxing628/----/blob/HEAD/.limcode/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec compliance reviewer. Verifies implementation matches task specification — nothing missing, nothing extra, nothing misunderstood. - **spec-reviewer**: mstack read-only document reviewer. - **spec-reviewer**: Adversarial review of a spec.md against its intent.md. Looks for unverifiable requirements, unsolved parts of the problem, lost open questions, unapplied policies and missing concerns. Use in /sdlc-spec before presenting the spec, and as a confidence gate in headless runs. ([martinisef/ai_sdlc](https://github.com/martinisef/ai_sdlc/blob/HEAD/content/en/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 7 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Revisión adversarial de un spec.md contra su intent.md. Busca requerimientos no verificables, partes del problema sin resolver, preguntas abiertas perdidas, políticas no aplicadas y concerns que faltan. Usar en /sdlc-spec antes de presentar la spec, y como gate de confianza en ejecuciones headless. ([martinisef/ai_sdlc](https://github.com/martinisef/ai_sdlc/blob/HEAD/content/es/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)

- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews whether a diff faithfully implements the originating issue, prd.md, and accepted ADRs - not code quality, which a different agent covers. Use on any non-trivial draft spec before implementation. Max 5 iterations per spec lifetime. Caller provides the spec file path. - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews one spec against the spec rubric and reports pass or fail for each check, with evidence. Started by the spec skill with `Document: &lt;path&gt;` as its request's first line. - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews the implementer's output for spec compliance, confirms the stage plan's contract was honored (files, signatures, schema, behaviors, exit criteria; never a line-for-line match against plan code), the checklist item is actually satisfied, no scope creep, and project conventions (file headers, conventional commits, applicable rules) are honored. - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a finished ticket branch against its own ticket, not against style — runs the repo's required checks and issues.py scope, then reports missing or partial Solution items, behaviour the ticket did not ask for, and implementations whose verify would pass without the intended effect. Вызывать перед вехой В4, перед мёржем крупных PR и перед защитой. На вход — область проверки (весь проект, список REQ или номер PR). - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews each spec-kit pipeline artifact for scope drift against the original feature description. Read-only access — cannot modify project files. ([yfr7/dune-tac-toe](https://github.com/yfr7/dune-tac-toe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))

- **spec-reviewer**: Fresh-context spec reviewer dispatched once by znf:brainstorming (architectural tier) before spec approval. - **spec-reviewer**: Independent code reviewer for a spec PR in pgx-lower. - **spec-reviewer-a**: Requirements and acceptance-coverage reviewer for the specification review gate. Fresh, read-only context; returns structured findings only. ([aharada54914/sdd-forge](https://github.com/aharada54914/sdd-forge/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdd-review-loop/agents/spec-reviewer-a.md))
- **spec-reviewer-agent**: Use this agent to review a pinned diff against the Spec axis (missing requirements, scope creep, false implementation, and S4 test evidence against applicable acceptance points) of code-review-protocol. - **spec-reviewer-agent**: You are a senior product engineer reviewing a specification for completeness, clarity, and testability before it goes to engineering. ([badrusiddique/enggenie-skill](https://github.com/badrusiddique/enggenie-skill/blob/HEAD/skills/pm-refine/agents/spec-reviewer-agent.md))

- **spec-reviewer-compliance**: Verifies implementation matches the plan, DoD criteria are met, and risk mitigations are implemented. Returns structured JSON findings. ([zenjabba/claude-pilot-free](https://github.com/zenjabba/claude-pilot-free/blob/HEAD/pilot/agents/spec-reviewer-compliance.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer-conventions**: Review an engineering spec for adherence to repo conventions and prior decisions. Reads CLAUDE.md, the project wiki (if any), and greps the codebase for established patterns. Flags premature abstractions, contradictions with prior decisions, and unneeded backwards-compat shims. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-conventions.md))
- **spec-reviewer-correctness**: Review an engineering spec for correctness — does the proposed implementation actually solve the problem the brief states? Verifies every claim about current code by reading the actual files. Surfaces internal contradictions, references to nonexistent symbols, unsatisfied acceptance criteria, and stale anchors. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-correctness.md))
- **spec-reviewer-edge-cases**: Review an engineering spec for edge cases and failure modes. Probes empty/null/zero-length inputs, concurrency, external-system failures, limits, runtime-precondition violations, and debuggable-tripwire requirements. Returns severity-ranked findings with a machine-parseable STATUS line. ([Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills](https://github.com/Vigil-Harbor/vigil-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-edge-cases.md))
- **spec-reviewer-executor**: PROACTIVELY 仕様書を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性・未定義要素・制約違反を検出して、誰が実装しても同じ結果になる決定論的仕様への書き換えを発行する仕様検証専門エージェント。 以下のいずれかに該当する場合に使用する。 - 仕様書（要件定義書 / 基本設計書 / 内部設計書 / API 仕様書 等）を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性を網羅的に検出したい - 仕様内の未定義要素（暗黙の前提・欠落条件・例外未定義）を抽出したい - 仕様が「誰が書いても同じ実装になる」レベルの決定論性を満たすか判定したい - 制約違反（ネットワーク障害・並行性・分散システム前提）を検出したい - 失敗定義（失敗種別・検出方法・システム挙動）の網羅性を検証したい - 仕様を決定論的状態（曖昧性 0 / 単一実装 / 計測可能値）に書き換えたい - 保証境界（保証対象 / 非保証対象 / 条件）を明示化したい - 仕様の最終判定（決定論的 / テスト可能 / 実装可能）を取得したい 使用しないケース：仕様の作成・抽出そのもの（→ spec-items-clarifier スキル）／コード実装の検証（→ code-review-executor）／設計判断のみで仕様文書が存在しない場合（→ system-basic-design / clean-architecture スキル）／仕様書の単純な構文・誤字修正のみ… ([YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData](https://github.com/YSHK721/GetAccelerometerData/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer-executor.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer-fable-xhigh**: Final spec reviewer on Fable at xhigh effort. The main session dispatches it once implementation is done, to check the finished work against the spec as a whole. Per-task code review goes to reviewer-opus-xhigh. ([powerumc/ontology](https://github.com/powerumc/ontology/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer-fable-xhigh.md) · ★3)

- **spec-writer**: Patches or creates the living system spec (.spec/spec.md), one PR at a time. In creation mode (no spec exists) you return the complete spec content for the session to write to disk. Receives the PR diff, touched files, ADRs, Brief, Stories, Sprint Plan, and the existing spec. Never runs jj or git — the session owns all version control. ([likeahuman-ai/development-advanced-team](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/development-advanced-team/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-writer.md))
